## Standing

When the queue is empty and an installment is due:

- New on Prime Video, Disney+, Hulu, HBO Max, Peacock for the next month (write around the 20th-25th).
- NFL week schedule and pick'em sheet, one per week through Week 18 (push Tuesday).
- Monthly important dates and printable month (next: August 2027).
- Planets visible and night sky (next: March 2027).
